   11:

   13:     lottery,
   14:     pageSize: 500,

   20:

    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: const { getAdminDb } = require("./statisticsEngine");
    4: 
    5: function normalizeLottery(lottery) {
    6:   return String(lottery || "PT_RIO").trim().toUpperCase();
    7: }
    8: 
    9: function normalizeGrupo(v) {
   10:   const n = Number(v);
   11:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 25 ? n : null;
   12: }
   13: 
   14: function normalizePosition(v) {
   15:   const n = Number(v);
   16:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= 1 && n <= 10 ? n : null;
   17: }
   18: 
   19: async function fetchPrizesForDrawRef(drawRef) {
   20:   const snap = await drawRef.collection("prizes").get();
   21: 
   22:   return snap.docs
   23:     .map((doc) => {
   24:       const data = doc.data() || {};
   25:       return {
   26:         id: doc.id,
   27:         ...data,
   28:         grupo: normalizeGrupo(data.grupo),
   29:         position: normalizePosition(data.position),
   30:       };
   31:     })
   32:     .filter((p) => p.grupo && p.position);
   33: }
   34: 
   35: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, concurrency, mapper) {
   36:   const arr = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
   37:   const out = new Array(arr.length);
   38:   let index = 0;
   39: 
   40:   async function worker() {
   41:     while (index < arr.length) {
   42:       const i = index++;
   43:       out[i] = await mapper(arr[i], i);
   44:     }
   45:   }
   46: 
   47:   await Promise.all(
   48:     Array.from({ length: Math.min(concurrency, arr.length) }, worker)
   49:   );
   50: 
   51:   return out;
   52: }
   53: 
   54: async function fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes({
   55:   lottery = "PT_RIO",
   56:   pageSize = 250,
   57:   prizeConcurrency = 12,
   58: } = {}) {
   59:   const db = getAdminDb();
   60:   const lk = normalizeLottery(lottery);
   61: 
   62:   const admin = require("firebase-admin");
   63:   const DOC_ID = admin.firestore.FieldPath.documentId();
   64: 
   65:   const draws = [];
   66:   let lastDoc = null;
   67:   let page = 0;
   68: 
   69:   while (true) {
   70:     page++;
   71: 
   72:     let q = db
   73:       .collection("draws")
   74:       .where("lottery_key", "==", lk)
   75:       .orderBy("ymd", "asc")
   76:       .orderBy(DOC_ID, "asc")
   77:       .limit(pageSize);
   78: 
   79:     if (lastDoc) q = q.startAfter(lastDoc);
   80: 
   81:     console.log(`[BOOTSTRAP] Buscando página ${page}...`);
   82: 
   83:     const snap = await q.get();
   84: 
   85:     if (snap.empty) break;
   86: 
   87:     const pageDraws = await mapWithConcurrency(
   88:       snap.docs,
   89:       prizeConcurrency,
   90:       async (doc) => {
   91:         const data = doc.data() || {};
   92:         const prizes = await fetchPrizesForDrawRef(doc.ref);
   93: 
   94:         return {
   95:           id: doc.id,
   96:           drawId: doc.id,
   97:           ...data,
   98:           prizes,
   99:         };
  100:       }
  101:     );
  102: 
  103:     draws.push(...pageDraws);
  104: 
  105:     console.log(
  106:       `[BOOTSTRAP] Página ${page}: ${snap.docs.length} draws | total=${draws.length}`
  107:     );
  108: 
  109:     lastDoc = snap.docs[snap.docs.length - 1];
  110: 
  111:     if (snap.docs.length < pageSize) break;
  112:   }
  113: 
  114:   return draws;
  115: }
  116: 
  117: module.exports = {
  118:   fetchAllDrawsWithPrizes,
  119: };

backend/engine/statisticsEngine.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    1: "use strict";
    2: 
    3: const { admin, getDb } = require("../service/firebaseAdmin");
    4: 
    5: function getAdminDb() {
    6:   return getDb();
    7: }
    8: 
    9: function safeArray(v) {
   10:   return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
   11: }
   12: 
   13: function normalizeUfKey(uf) {
   14:   return String(uf || "PT_RIO").trim().toUpperCase() || "PT_RIO";
   15: }
   16: 
   17: function snapshotRef(db, uf, scope = "dashboard_full") {
   18:   return db
   19:     .collection("statistics")
   20:     .doc(normalizeUfKey(uf))
   21:     .collection("snapshots")
   22:     .doc(scope);
   23: }
   24: 
   25: async function readStatisticsSnapshot({ uf = "PT_RIO", scope = "dashboard_full" } = {}) {
   26:   const db = getAdminDb();
   27:   const snap = await snapshotRef(db, uf, scope).get();
   28: 
   29:   if (!snap.exists) {
   30:     return { exists: false, data: null };
   31:   }
   32: 
   33:   return { exists: true, data: snap.data() };
   34: }
   35: 
   36: async function writeStatisticsSnapshot({
   37:   uf = "PT_RIO",
   38:   scope = "dashboard_full",
   39:   data,
   40: } = {}) {
   41:   const db = getAdminDb();
   42: 
   43:   const payload = {
   44:     ...data,
   45:     uf: normalizeUfKey(uf),
   46:     scope,
   47:     updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
   48:   };
   49: 
   50:   await snapshotRef(db, uf, scope).set(payload, { merge: true });
   51: 
   52:   return payload;
   53: }
   54: 
   55: module.exports = {
   56:   getAdminDb,
   57:   safeArray,
   58:   normalizeUfKey,
   59:   readStatisticsSnapshot,
   60:   writeStatisticsSnapshot,
   61: };

    1: // src/utils/buildRanking.js

    3: /**
    4:  * buildRanking (PalPitaco)
    5:  *
    6:  * Padrão atual (mantido):
    7:  * - "Aparições" = total de ocorrências por prize (posição).
    8:  *   Ex.: se o draw tem 7 posições, pode contar até 7 ocorrências por draw.
    9:  *
   10:  * Opção futura (sem ativar por padrão):
   11:  * - countMode: "byDraw" => conta no máximo 1 ocorrência por grupo dentro do mesmo draw.
   12:  */
   13:

   38:   { grupo: 21, animal: "TOURO", slug: "touro", dezenas: ["81", "82", "83", "84"] },
   39:   { grupo: 22, animal: "TIGRE", slug: "tigre", dezenas: ["85", "86", "87", "88"] },
   40:   { grupo: 23, animal: "URSO", slug: "urso", dezenas: ["89", "90", "91", "92"] },
   41:   { grupo: 24, animal: "VEADO", slug: "veado", dezenas: ["93", "94", "95", "96"] },
   42:   { grupo: 25, animal: "VACA", slug: "vaca", dezenas: ["97", "98", "99", "00"] },
   43: ];
   44: 
   45: const BY_GRUPO = new Map(BICHO_MAP.map((b) => [b.grupo, b]));
   46: 
   47: /* =========================
   48:    Utils
   49: ========================= */
   50: function pad2(n) {
   51:   return String(n).padStart(2, "0");
   52: }
   53: 
   54: function normalizeAnimalLocal(a) {
   55:   return String(a || "")
   56:     .trim()
   57:     .toUpperCase()
   58:     .normalize("NFD")
   59:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   60:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   61:     .trim();
   62: }
   63: 
   64: function getOfficialByGrupo(grupoNum) {
   65:   const g = Number(grupoNum);
   66:   if (!Number.isFinite(g)) return null;
   67:   const b = BY_GRUPO.get(g);
   68:   if (!b) return null;
   69: 
   70:   return {
   71:     grupoNum: g,
   72:     grupo: pad2(g),
   73:     animal: b.animal ? normalizeAnimalLocal(b.animal) : "",
   74:     slug: b.slug || "",
   75:     dezenas: Array.isArray(b.dezenas) ? b.dezenas.slice() : [],
   76:   };
   77: }
   78: 
   79: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
   80:   if (!input) return null;
   81: 
   82:   // Firestore Timestamp com toDate()
   83:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
   84:     const d = input.toDate();
   85:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
   86:       return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
   87:     }
   88:   }
   89: 
   90:   // Timestamp-like { seconds } / { _seconds }
   91:   if (
   92:     typeof input === "object" &&
   93:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) || Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
   94:   ) {
   95:     const sec = Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds))
   96:       ? Number(input.seconds)
   97:       : Number(input._seconds);
   98: 
   99:     const d = new Date(sec * 1000);
  100:     if (!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
  101:       return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
  102:     }
  103:   }
  104: 
  105:   // Date
  106:   if (input instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(input.getTime())) {
  107:     return `${input.getFullYear()}-${pad2(input.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(input.getDate())}`;
  108:   }
  109: 
  110:   const s = String(input).trim();
  111:   if (!s) return null;
  112: 
  113:   // YYYY-MM-DD...
  114:   const iso = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
  115:   if (iso) return `${iso[1]}-${iso[2]}-${iso[3]}`;
  116: 
  117:   // DD/MM/YYYY
  118:   const br = s.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);
  119:   if (br) return `${br[3]}-${br[2]}-${br[1]}`;
  120: 
  121:   return null;
  122: }
  123: 
  124: function getDrawYmdRaw(d) {
  125:   if (!d) return null;
  126:   return d.date ?? d.ymd ?? d.draw_date ?? d.close_date ?? d.data ?? d.dt ?? null;
  127: }
  128: 
  129: function getDrawHourRaw(d) {
  130:   if (!d) return "";
  131:   return String(d.close_hour ?? d.closeHour ?? d.hour ?? d.hora ?? "").trim();
  132: }
  133: 
  134: /**
  135:  * ✅ DEDUP DE DRAWS (CORREÇÃO CRÍTICA)
  136:  * - Prioriza a chave LÓGICA (ymd + close_hour) para eliminar duplicidade real
  137:  * - Só cai para id/drawId se NÃO conseguir montar (ymd/hour)
  138:  * - Se ainda assim ficar sem chave, usa fallback com índice (não colapsa tudo em "__")
  139:  */
  140: function dedupeDraws(draws) {
  141:   const arr = Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : [];
  142:   if (!arr.length) return arr;
  143: 
  144:   const seen = new Set();
  145:   const out = [];
  146: 
  147:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
  148:     const d = arr[i];
  149: 
  150:     const ymd =
  151:       String(d?.ymd || "").trim() ||
  152:       normalizeToYMD(getDrawYmdRaw(d)) ||
  153:       "";
  154: 
  155:     const hour = getDrawHourRaw(d);
  156: 
  157:     const logicalKey = ymd && hour ? `${ymd}__${hour}` : "";
  158:     const fallbackKey =
  159:       (d?.drawId != null && String(d.drawId)) ||
  160:       (d?.id != null && String(d.id)) ||
  161:       "";
  162: 
  163:     const key = logicalKey || fallbackKey || `${ymd || "nodate"}__${hour || "nohour"}__idx${i}`;
  164: 
  165:     if (seen.has(key)) continue;
  166:     seen.add(key);
  167:     out.push(d);
  168:   }
  169: 
  170:   return out;
  171: }
  172: 
  173: /**
  174:  * ✅ Dedup defensivo de prizes dentro do mesmo draw
  175:  * - prioridade: prizeId (se existir)
  176:  * - fallback: position + grupo + animal
  177:  * - se position vier vazio, ainda assim diferencia por "idx" para não colapsar tudo
  178:  */
  179: function dedupePrizes(prizes) {
  180:   const arr = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes : [];
  181:   if (!arr.length) return arr;
  182: 
  183:   const seen = new Set();
  184:   const out = [];
  185: 
  186:   for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i += 1) {
  187:     const p = arr[i];
  188: 
  189:     const grupoRaw = p?.grupo ?? p?.group ?? "";
  190:     const animalRaw = p?.animal ?? "";
  191:     const posRaw = p?.posicao ?? p?.position ?? "";
  192: 
  193:     const baseFallback = `${String(posRaw)}__${String(grupoRaw)}__${normalizeAnimalLocal(animalRaw)}`;
  194:     const key =
  195:       (p?.prizeId != null && String(p.prizeId)) ||
  196:       (String(posRaw).trim() ? baseFallback : `${baseFallback}__idx${i}`);
  197: 
  198:     if (seen.has(key)) continue;
  199:     seen.add(key);
  200:     out.push(p);
  201:   }
  202: 
  203:   return out;
  204: }
  205: 
  206: /* =========================
  207:    Main
  208: ========================= */
  209: export function buildRanking(draws, opts = {}) {
  210:   // ✅ correção: dedup no nível de draws ANTES de qualquer métrica
  211:   const list = dedupeDraws(Array.isArray(draws) ? draws : []);
  212: 
  213:   // Padrão atual (mantido): conta por prize
  214:   const countMode = String(opts.countMode || "byPrize"); // "byPrize" | "byDraw"
  215:   const maxIntegrityIssues = Number.isFinite(Number(opts.maxIntegrityIssues))
  216:     ? Math.max(0, Number(opts.maxIntegrityIssues))
  217:     : 300;
  218: 
  219:   const totalDraws = list.length;
  220: 
  221:   // metas de período
  222:   const daySet = new Set();
  223:   let minDate = null;
  224:   let maxDate = null;
  225: 
  226:   for (const d of list) {
  227:     const ymd = normalizeToYMD(getDrawYmdRaw(d));
  228:     if (!ymd) continue;
  229: 
  230:     daySet.add(ymd);
  231:     if (!minDate || ymd < minDate) minDate = ymd;
  232:     if (!maxDate || ymd > maxDate) maxDate = ymd;
  233:   }
  234: 
  235:   const uniqueDays = daySet.size;
  236: 
  237:   // ranking
  238:   const map = new Map();
  239:   let totalOcorrencias = 0;
  240: 
  241:   // auditoria de consistência grupo x animal
  242:   const integrityIssues = [];
  243: 
  244:   for (const draw of list) {
  245:     const prizes = dedupePrizes(draw?.prizes);
  246: 
  247:     // se countMode = byDraw, evitamos contar o mesmo grupo mais de 1x por draw
  248:     const countedGroupsThisDraw = countMode === "byDraw" ? new Set() : null;
  249: 
  250:     for (const prize of prizes) {
  251:       const grupoRaw = prize?.grupo ?? prize?.group;
  252:       const animalRaw = prize?.animal;
  253: 
  254:       if (grupoRaw == null) continue;
  255: 
  256:       const grupoNum = Number(grupoRaw);
  257:       if (!Number.isFinite(grupoNum)) continue;
  258:       if (grupoNum < 1 || grupoNum > 25) continue;
  259: 
  260:       if (countedGroupsThisDraw) {
  261:         const k = String(grupoNum);
  262:         if (countedGroupsThisDraw.has(k)) continue;
  263:         countedGroupsThisDraw.add(k);
  264:       }
  265: 
  266:       const official = getOfficialByGrupo(grupoNum);
  267:       const grupo = official?.grupo || pad2(grupoNum);
  268: 
  269:       const animalFromData = animalRaw ? normalizeAnimalLocal(animalRaw) : "";
  270:       const animalOfficial = official?.animal || "";
  271:       const animalLabel = animalFromData || animalOfficial || "";
  272: 
  273:       let entry = map.get(grupo);
  274:       if (!entry) {
  275:         entry = {
  276:           grupo,                 // "01".."25" (string)
  277:           grupoNum: grupoNum,    // 1..25 (number)
  278:           animal: animalLabel,   // "GATO"
  279:           slug: official?.slug || "",
  280:           dezenas: official?.dezenas || [],
  281:           total: 0,
  282:         };
  283:         map.set(grupo, entry);
  284:       } else {
  285:         // completa metadados se necessário
  286:         if (!entry.grupoNum) entry.grupoNum = grupoNum;
  287:         if (!entry.slug && official?.slug) entry.slug = official.slug;
  288:         if ((!entry.dezenas || !entry.dezenas.length) && official?.dezenas?.length) {
  289:           entry.dezenas = official.dezenas;
  290:         }
  291:         if (!entry.animal && animalLabel) entry.animal = animalLabel;
  292:       }
  293: 
  294:       // auditoria: se veio animal no dado e diverge do oficial, registra e “trava” no oficial
  295:       if (
  296:         animalFromData &&
  297:         animalOfficial &&
  298:         animalFromData !== animalOfficial &&
  299:         integrityIssues.length < maxIntegrityIssues
  300:       ) {
  301:         integrityIssues.push({
  302:           drawId: draw?.drawId ?? draw?.id ?? null,
  303:           date: getDrawYmdRaw(draw),
  304:           close_hour: getDrawHourRaw(draw) || null,
  305:           grupo,
  306:           grupoNum,
  307:           animal_no_dado: animalRaw,
  308:           animal_normalizado: animalFromData,
  309:           animal_oficial: animalOfficial,
  310:         });
  311: 
  312:         if (animalOfficial) entry.animal = animalOfficial;
  313:       }
  314: 
  315:       entry.total += 1;
  316:       totalOcorrencias += 1;
  317:     }
  318:   }
  319: 
  320:   // garante 01..25 sempre presentes
  321:   for (let i = 1; i <= 25; i += 1) {
  322:     const official = getOfficialByGrupo(i);
  323:     const g = official?.grupo || pad2(i);
  324: 
  325:     let entry = map.get(g);
  326: 
  327:     if (!entry) {
  328:       map.set(g, {
  329:         grupo: g,
  330:         grupoNum: i,
  331:         animal: official?.animal || "",
  332:         slug: official?.slug || "",
  333:         dezenas: official?.dezenas || [],
  334:         total: 0,
  335:       });
  336:     } else {
  337:       if (!entry.grupoNum) entry.grupoNum = i;
  338:       if (!entry.animal) entry.animal = official?.animal || "";
  339:       if (!entry.slug) entry.slug = official?.slug || "";
  340:       if ((!entry.dezenas || !entry.dezenas.length) && official?.dezenas?.length) {
  341:         entry.dezenas = official.dezenas;
  342:       }
  343:     }
  344:   }
  345: 
  346:   const ranking = Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => {
  347:     const dt = Number(b.total) - Number(a.total);
  348:     if (dt !== 0) return dt;
  349:     return String(a.grupo).localeCompare(String(b.grupo));
  350:   });
  351: 
  352:   return {
  353:     ranking,
  354:     top3: ranking.slice(0, 3),
  355:     totalOcorrencias,
  356:     integrityIssues,
  357:     totalDraws,
  358:     uniqueDays,
  359:     minDate,
  360:     maxDate,
  361: 
  362:     // útil para a próxima etapa (definição de regra)
  363:     countMode,
  364:   };
  365: }

src/utils/scoreEngine.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    1: export function applyScoreEngine(ranking = [], opts = {}) {
    2:   const weights = {
    3:     aparicoes: 1,
    4:     ...opts.weights,
    5:   };
    6: 
    7:   const scored = (Array.isArray(ranking) ? ranking : []).map((item) => {
    8:     const aparicoes = Number(item.total ?? item.apar ?? 0) || 0;
    9: 
   10:     const score =
   11:       aparicoes * Number(weights.aparicoes || 0);
   12: 
   13:     return {
   14:       ...item,
   15:       score,
   16:       scoreDetails: {
   17:         aparicoes,
   18:         weights,
   19:       },
   20:     };
   21:   });
   22: 
   23:   scored.sort((a, b) => {
   24:     const ds = Number(b.score || 0) - Number(a.score || 0);
   25:     if (ds !== 0) return ds;
   26:     return Number(a.grupoNum || a.grupo || 0) - Number(b.grupoNum || b.grupo || 0);
   27:   });
   28: 
   29:   return scored;
   30: }
